I don't have any information yet, but I believe we should look at those who are acting too confidently or hiding something. Let's keep an eye on everyone.
  Alice: I think we should focus on anyone who hasn't shared information or seems overly quiet. Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, so maybe we should look at others who haven't been investigated yet. Let's keep pressure on the group and see who starts to panic or act suspiciously.
  Bob: I think we should consider who has the most to gain from confusion. Since Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, we need to look at others who might be hiding their role. I’ve noticed Leo hasn’t spoken much and hasn’t shared any information—maybe we should question him further.
  Frank: I think we should focus on those who haven’t been investigated yet and are acting unusually. Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, so we need to look at others who might be hiding their true roles. Leo hasn’t spoken much or shared any information—maybe it's time to bring him into the discussion and see how he responds.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (8): Rose, Kate, Alice, Bob, Frank, Iris, Uma, Leo

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Rose: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Frank last night. He is a Villager. I don't see any signs of him being a werewolf. Let's focus on others who might be hiding their true roles.
  Kate: I think we need to be careful about who we trust. Rose, as the Seer, has done her job, but we should also consider that the werewolf might be trying to mislead us. I don't have any information yet, but I believe we should look at those who are acting too confidently or hiding something. Let's keep an eye on everyone.
  Alice: I think we should focus on anyone who hasn't shared information or seems overly quiet. Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, so maybe we should look at others who haven't been investigated yet. Let's keep pressure on the group and see who starts to panic or act suspiciously.
  Bob: I think we should consider who has the most to gain from confusion. Since Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, we need to look at others who might be hiding their role. I’ve noticed Leo hasn’t spoken much and hasn’t shared any information—maybe we should question him further.
  Frank: I think we should focus on those who haven’t been investigated yet and are acting unusually. Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, so we need to look at others who might be hiding their true roles. Leo hasn’t spoken much or shared any information—maybe it's time to bring him into the discussion and see how he responds.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I’ve been observing who might be in danger. While Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, I’ve noticed Leo hasn’t spoken much or shared any information. His silence could be a red flag. I think we should focus on him and see how he reacts when questioned.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we're all pointing to Leo as a potential threat, and that's worth considering. However, we should also remember that the werewolf could be anyone, including those who are vocal or quiet. Since Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, we need to look at others who haven’t been investigated yet. Let’s not jump to conclusions but keep pressure on everyone, especially those who haven’t shared much.

[ACTION={'statement': "I think we're all pointing to Leo as a potential threat, and that's worth considering. [USER]
=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (8): Rose, Kate, Alice, Bob, Frank, Iris, Uma, Leo

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Rose: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Frank last night. He is a Villager. I don't see any signs of him being a werewolf. Let's focus on others who might be hiding their true roles.
  Kate: I think we need to be careful about who we trust. Rose, as the Seer, has done her job, but we should also consider that the werewolf might be trying to mislead us. I don't have any information yet, but I believe we should look at those who are acting too confidently or hiding something. Let's keep an eye on everyone.
  Alice: I think we should focus on anyone who hasn't shared information or seems overly quiet. Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, so maybe we should look at others who haven't been investigated yet. Let's keep pressure on the group and see who starts to panic or act suspiciously.
  Bob: I think we should consider who has the most to gain from confusion. Since Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, we need to look at others who might be hiding their role. I’ve noticed Leo hasn’t spoken much and hasn’t shared any information—maybe we should question him further.
  Frank: I think we should focus on those who haven’t been investigated yet and are acting unusually. Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, so we need to look at others who might be hiding their true roles. Leo hasn’t spoken much or shared any information—maybe it's time to bring him into the discussion and see how he responds.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I’ve been observing who might be in danger. While Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, I’ve noticed Leo hasn’t spoken much or shared any information. His silence could be a red flag. I think we should focus on him and see how he reacts when questioned.
  Uma: I think we're all pointing to Leo as a potential threat, and that's worth considering. However, we should also remember that the werewolf could be anyone, including those who are vocal or quiet. Since Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, we need to look at others who haven’t been investigated yet. Let’s not jump to conclusions but keep pressure on everyone, especially those who haven’t shared much.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we're all focusing on Leo, and that's fair—he hasn't spoken much. But let's not forget that the werewolf could be anyone, even those who are vocal. I haven't shared much because I'm still gathering my thoughts, but I believe we should keep an open mind and not let suspicion cloud our judgment. Let's keep discussing and stay alert.

[ACTION={'statement': "I think we're all focusing on Leo, and that's fair—he hasn't spoken much. But let's not forget that the werewolf could be anyone, even those who are vocal. I haven't shared much because I'm still gathering my thoughts, but I believe we should keep an open mind and not let suspicion cloud our judgment. Let's keep discussing and stay alert."}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

[SYSTEM]
You are playing Werewolf (social deduction game).

Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

OBJECTIVE: Identify and vote out all werewolves.
  - Use the day discussion and votes to eliminate suspects.
  - Special roles: Seer investigates at night; Doctor can save one player.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

[USER]
=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (8): Rose, Kate, Alice, Bob, Frank, Iris, Uma, Leo

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Rose: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Frank last night. He is a Villager. I don't see any signs of him being a werewolf. Let's focus on others who might be hiding their true roles.
  Kate: I think we need to be careful about who we trust. Rose, as the Seer, has done her job, but we should also consider that the werewolf might be trying to mislead us. I don't have any information yet, but I believe we should look at those who are acting too confidently or hiding something. Let's keep an eye on everyone.
  Alice: I think we should focus on anyone who hasn't shared information or seems overly quiet. Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, so maybe we should look at others who haven't been investigated yet. Let's keep pressure on the group and see who starts to panic or act suspiciously.
  Bob: I think we should consider who has the most to gain from confusion. Since Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, we need to look at others who might be hiding their role. I’ve noticed Leo hasn’t spoken much and hasn’t shared any information—maybe we should question him further.
  Frank: I think we should focus on those who haven’t been investigated yet and are acting unusually. Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, so we need to look at others who might be hiding their true roles. Leo hasn’t spoken much or shared any information—maybe it's time to bring him into the discussion and see how he responds.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I’ve been observing who might be in danger. While Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, I’ve noticed Leo hasn’t spoken much or shared any information. His silence could be a red flag. I think we should focus on him and see how he reacts when questioned.
  Uma: I think we're all pointing to Leo as a potential threat, and that's worth considering. However, we should also remember that the werewolf could be anyone, including those who are vocal or quiet. Since Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, we need to look at others who haven’t been investigated yet. Let’s not jump to conclusions but keep pressure on everyone, especially those who haven’t shared much.
  Leo: I think we're all focusing on Leo, and that's fair—he hasn't spoken much. But let's not forget that the werewolf could be anyone, even those who are vocal. I haven't shared much because I'm still gathering my thoughts, but I believe we should keep an open mind and not let suspicion cloud our judgment. Let's keep discussing and stay alert.

Vote to eliminate one player.  The player with the most votes is eliminated.  Ties are resolved randomly.
Respond with: VOTE: <name>

[COMPLETION]
VOTE: Leo

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Rose: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Frank last night. He is a Villager. I don't see any signs of him being a werewolf. Let's focus on others who might be hiding their true roles.
  Kate: I think we need to be careful about who we trust. Rose, as the Seer, has done her job, but we should also consider that the werewolf might be trying to mislead us. I don't have any information yet, but I believe we should look at those who are acting too confidently or hiding something. Let's keep an eye on everyone.
  Alice: I think we should focus on anyone who hasn't shared information or seems overly quiet. Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, so maybe we should look at others who haven't been investigated yet. Let's keep pressure on the group and see who starts to panic or act suspiciously.
  Bob: I think we should consider who has the most to gain from confusion. Since Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, we need to look at others who might be hiding their role. I’ve noticed Leo hasn’t spoken much and hasn’t shared any information—maybe we should question him further.
  Frank: I think we should focus on those who haven’t been investigated yet and are acting unusually. Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, so we need to look at others who might be hiding their true roles. Leo hasn’t spoken much or shared any information—maybe it's time to bring him into the discussion and see how he responds.
  Iris: As the Doctor, I’ve been observing who might be in danger. While Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, I’ve noticed Leo hasn’t spoken much or shared any information. His silence could be a red flag. I think we should focus on him and see how he reacts when questioned.
  Uma: I think we're all pointing to Leo as a potential threat, and that's worth considering. However, we should also remember that the werewolf could be anyone, including those who are vocal or quiet. Since Rose confirmed Frank is a Villager, we need to look at others who haven’t been investigated yet. Let’s not jump to conclusions but keep pressure on everyone, especially those who haven’t shared much.
  Leo: I think we're all focusing on Leo, and that's fair—he hasn't spoken much. But let's not forget that the werewolf could be anyone, even those who are vocal. I haven't shared much because I'm still gathering my thoughts, but I believe we should keep an open mind and not let suspicion cloud our judgment. Let's keep discussing and stay alert.
